11    /// The shortest text that reads back as @p x, so a bound such as
12    /// 0.8999999999999999 does not show as 0.9.
13    inline std::string shortest_text(float64_t x)
14    {
15      // std::to_chars for floating point needs macOS 13.3, and wheels target
16      // macOS 11, so try increasing precision until the text reads back.
17      std::array<char, 32> buffer;
18      for (int precision = 1; precision <= 17; ++precision)
19      {
20        std::snprintf(buffer.data(), buffer.size(), "%.*g", precision, x);
21        if (std::strtod(buffer.data(), nullptr) == x)
22        {
23          break;
24        }
25      }
26
27      // %g turns to scientific notation once the exponent reaches the
28      // precision, so 906680 shows as 9.0668e+05. Like std::to_chars, use
29      // fixed notation when it is no longer.
30      std::string text = buffer.data();
31      const auto e = text.find('e');
32      if (e != std::string::npos)
33      {
34        const int exponent = std::atoi(text.c_str() + e + 1);
35        if (exponent >= 0 && (x < 0) + exponent + 1 <= static_cast<int>(text.size()))
36        {
37          std::snprintf(buffer.data(), buffer.size(), "%.0f", x);
38          text = buffer.data();
39        }
40      }
41      return text;
42    }
43
44    /// The queries in @p t in row-major order, and their positions sorted by
45    /// time. A NaN sorts first, so checking the first and last sorted queries
46    /// against a domain rejects it.
47    template <typename Q>
48    struct SortedQueries
49    {
50      std::vector<Q> values;
51      std::vector<size_t> order;
52    };
53
54    template <typename Q>
55    SortedQueries<Q> sort_queries(const Tensor<Q>& t)
56    {
57      SortedQueries<Q> queries;
58      walk(t, [&](const std::vector<size_t>& index) { queries.values.push_back(t(index)); });
59      queries.order.resize(queries.values.size());
60      std::iota(queries.order.begin(), queries.order.end(), size_t{0});
61      const auto& v = queries.values;
62      std::sort(queries.order.begin(), queries.order.end(), [&](size_t a, size_t b) {
63        if constexpr (std::is_floating_point_v<Q>)
64        {
65          return std::isnan(v[a]) ? !std::isnan(v[b]) : v[a] < v[b];
66        }
67        else
68        {
69          return v[a] < v[b];
70        }
71      });
72      return queries;
73    }
74  }

76  template <typename T>
77  Channel<T>::Channel(Times times, const Tensor<T>& values, float64_t snapTolerance)
78  {
79    set_snap_tolerance(snapTolerance);
80    if (values.rank() == 0)
81    {
82      throw std::invalid_argument("Values must have a time axis");
83    }
84    if (values.shape(0) != times.size())
85    {
86      throw std::invalid_argument(
87          "Expected one timestamp per observation: got " + std::to_string(times.size())
88          + " timestamps for " + std::to_string(values.shape(0)) + " observations");
89    }
90
91    auto storage = std::make_shared<ChannelStorage<T>>(ChannelStorage<T>{
92        std::move(times),
93        std::vector<size_t>(values.shape().begin() + 1, values.shape().end()),
94        {}});
95    storage->values.reserve(values.size());
96    walk(values, [&](const std::vector<size_t>& index) {
97      storage->values.push_back(detail::store_copy(values(index)));
98    });
99    m_storage = std::move(storage);
100    m_shape = m_storage->shape;
101  }
102
103  template <typename T>
104  Channel<T>::Channel(Times times, std::vector<size_t> shape, std::vector<T> values, float64_t snapTolerance)
105  {
106    set_snap_tolerance(snapTolerance);
107    const size_t blockSize = std::accumulate(shape.begin(), shape.end(), size_t{1}, std::multiplies<>());
108    if (values.size() != times.size() * blockSize)
109    {
110      throw std::invalid_argument(
111          "Expected " + std::to_string(times.size() * blockSize) + " values for " + std::to_string(times.size())
112          + " observations, got " + std::to_string(values.size()));
113    }
114    m_storage = std::make_shared<ChannelStorage<T>>(
115        ChannelStorage<T>{std::move(times), std::move(shape), std::move(values)});
116    m_shape = m_storage->shape;
117  }
118
119  template <typename T>
120  void Channel<T>::set_snap_tolerance(float64_t tolerance)
121  {
122    if (std::isnan(tolerance) || tolerance < 0 || tolerance >= 1)
123    {
124      throw std::invalid_argument("The snap tolerance must be in [0, 1)");
125    }
126    m_snapTolerance = tolerance;
127  }

129  template <typename T>
130  Channel<T> Channel<T>::view_lagged_at(Times times, std::span<const float64_t> lags) const
131  {
132    if (lags.empty())
133    {
134      throw std::invalid_argument("Cannot stack zero lags");
135    }
136    const auto& at = times.values();
137    if (!at.empty() && size() == 0)
138    {
139      throw std::invalid_argument("Cannot view a channel without observations at a time");
140    }
141    // Every component of observation k reads this channel at or before its
142    // observation at times[k] minus the smallest lag, the base.
143    const auto smallest = *std::min_element(lags.begin(), lags.end());
144    auto base = std::make_shared<std::vector<size_t>>();
145    base->reserve(at.size());
146    size_t hint = 0;
147    for (const auto t : at)
148    {
149      hint = index_at(t - smallest, hint);
150      base->push_back(hint);
151    }
152
153    Channel view = *this;
154    view.m_storage = nullptr;
155    view.m_source = std::make_shared<const Channel>(*this);
156    view.m_times = std::make_shared<const Times>(std::move(times));
157    view.m_base = std::move(base);
158    view.m_lags.assign(lags.begin(), lags.end());
159    view.m_shape.insert(view.m_shape.begin(), lags.size());
160    // A closed end at the new last timestamp is dropped, as until drops it.
161    return view.m_end ? view.until(*view.m_end, view.m_endClosed) : view;
162  }
163
164  template <typename T>
165  Channel<T> Channel<T>::view_at_indices(std::vector<size_t> indices) const
166  {
167    std::vector<float64_t> at;
168    at.reserve(indices.size());
169    for (size_t k = 0; k < indices.size(); ++k)
170    {
171      if (indices[k] >= size())
172      {
173        throw std::out_of_range(
174            "Observation index " + std::to_string(indices[k]) + " is out of range for " + std::to_string(size())
175            + " observations");
176      }
177      if (k > 0 && indices[k] <= indices[k - 1])
178      {
179        throw std::invalid_argument("Observation indices must be strictly increasing");
180      }
181      at.push_back(time(indices[k]));
182    }
183
184    // One component at lag 0, whose base is the given index. A view with
185    // one component reads observation i of its source at its base i, so a
186    // view of it takes from that source directly: chains of views stay one
187    // level deep, and the views between are freed.
188    Channel view = *this;
189    view.m_storage = nullptr;
190    if (m_source && m_lags.size() == 1)
191    {
192      for (auto& i : indices)
193      {
194        i = (*m_base)[i];
195      }
196      view.m_source = m_source;
197    }
198    else
199    {
200      view.m_source = std::make_shared<const Channel>(*this);
201    }
202    view.m_times = std::make_shared<const Times>(times_like(std::move(at)));
203    view.m_base = std::make_shared<const std::vector<size_t>>(std::move(indices));
204    view.m_lags = {0.0};
205    view.m_end = std::nullopt;
206    view.m_endClosed = false;
207    view.m_startClosed = true;
208    return view;
209  }
210
211  template <typename T>
212  Channel<T> Channel<T>::with_times(Times times) const
213  {
214    if (m_lags.size() > 1)
215    {
216      throw std::logic_error("A view with several lags finds its observations from its times; it cannot move them");
217    }
218    if (times.size() != size())
219    {
220      throw std::invalid_argument("Expected one time per observation: got " + std::to_string(times.size())
221                                  + " times for " + std::to_string(size()) + " observations");
222    }
223    auto channel = *this;
224    channel.m_times = std::make_shared<const Times>(std::move(times));
225    channel.m_end = std::nullopt;
226    channel.m_endClosed = false;
227    return channel;
228  }
229
230  template <typename T>
231  Channel<T> Channel<T>::until(float64_t end, bool closed) const
232  {
233    const auto atLast = size() > 0 && end == time(size() - 1);
234    if (std::isnan(end) || (size() > 0 && end < time(size() - 1)) || (atLast && !closed))
235    {
236      throw std::invalid_argument("The end of a channel must come after its last timestamp");
237    }
238    auto channel = *this;
239    channel.m_end = atLast ? std::nullopt : std::optional(end);
240    channel.m_endClosed = closed;
241    return channel;
242  }
243
244  template <typename T>
245  Channel<T> Channel<T>::to(TimeUnit from, TimeUnit to) const
246  {
247    if (from == to)
248    {
249      return *this;
250    }
251    auto channel = *this;
252    channel.m_times = std::make_shared<const Times>(held_times().to(from, to));
253    if (m_end)
254    {
255      channel.m_end = convert_time(*m_end, from, to);
256    }
257    for (auto& lag : channel.m_lags)
258    {
259      lag = convert_time(lag, from, to);
260    }
261    // A view with several lags reads its source at its own times less its
262    // lags, so the source moves to the same unit. One with one lag reads it
263    // by index only, so it stays as it is.
264    if (m_source && m_lags.size() > 1)
265    {
266      channel.m_source = std::make_shared<const Channel>(m_source->to(from, to));
267    }
268    return channel.with_end_after_last();
269  }
270
271  template <typename T>
272  Channel<T> Channel<T>::shifted(float64_t delta) const
273  {
274    auto channel = *this;
275    auto values = held_times().values();
276    for (auto& value : values)
277    {
278      value += delta;
279    }
280    detail::keep_increasing(values);
281    channel.m_times = std::make_shared<const Times>(held_times().with_values(std::move(values)));
282    if (m_end)
283    {
284      channel.m_end = *m_end + delta;
285    }
286    // A view with several lags reads its source at its own times less its
287    // lags, so the source shifts alike. One with one lag reads it by index
288    // only, so it stays as it is.
289    if (m_source && m_lags.size() > 1)
290    {
291      channel.m_source = std::make_shared<const Channel>(m_source->shifted(delta));
292    }
293    return channel.with_end_after_last();
294  }
295
296  template <typename T>
297  Channel<T> Channel<T>::with_end_after_last() const
298  {
299    if (!m_end || size() == 0 || *m_end > time(size() - 1))
300    {
301      return *this;
302    }
303    auto channel = *this;
304    channel.m_end = m_endClosed ? std::nullopt
305                                : std::optional(std::nextafter(time(size() - 1),
306                                                               std::numeric_limits<float64_t>::infinity()));
307    return channel;
308  }

310  template <typename T>
311  Channel<T> Channel<T>::with_start_closed(bool closed) const
312  {
313    auto channel = *this;
314    channel.m_startClosed = closed;
315    return channel;
316  }

318  template <typename T>
319  bool Channel<T>::operator==(const Channel& other) const
320  {
321    return same_observations(other) && held_times() == other.held_times();
322  }
323
324  template <typename T>
325  bool Channel<T>::same_observations(const Channel& other) const
326  {
327    return m_snapTolerance == other.m_snapTolerance && m_shape == other.m_shape
328           && m_end == other.m_end && end_closed() == other.end_closed() && m_startClosed == other.m_startClosed
329           && held_times().values() == other.held_times().values() && values() == other.values();
330  }

332  template <typename T>
333  Tensor<T> Channel<T>::lookup(float64_t t) const
334  {
335    Tensor<T> out(m_shape);
336    const float64_t times[] = {t};
337    const size_t order[] = {0};
338    lookup_sorted(times, order, out.data());
339    return out;
340  }

342  template <typename T>
343  template <std::random_access_iterator Out>
344  void Channel<T>::lookup_sorted(std::span<const float64_t> sortedTimes, std::span<const size_t> order, Out out) const
345  {
346    const auto blockSize = block_size();
347    const auto indices = indices_sorted(sortedTimes);
348    for (size_t k = 0; k < sortedTimes.size(); ++k)
349    {
350      copy_observation(indices[k], out + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(order[k] * blockSize));
351    }
352  }

354  template <typename T>
355  typename std::vector<T>::const_reference Channel<T>::flat_value(size_t i) const
356  {
357    if (!m_source)
358    {
359      return m_storage->values[i];
360    }
361    const auto blockSize = block_size();
362    const auto sourceBlock = blockSize / m_lags.size();
363    const auto r = i % blockSize;
364    return m_source->flat_value(source_index(i / blockSize, r / sourceBlock) * sourceBlock + r % sourceBlock);
365  }

367  template <typename T>
368  Tensor<T> Channel<T>::values() const
369  {
370    std::vector<size_t> fullShape{size()};
371    fullShape.insert(fullShape.end(), shape().begin(), shape().end());
372    Tensor<T> out(fullShape);
373    if (size() > 0)
374    {
375      const auto blockSize = block_size();
376      for (size_t k = 0; k < size(); ++k)
377      {
378        copy_observation(k, out.data() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(k * blockSize));
379      }
380    }
381    return out;
382  }
383
384  template <typename T>
385  Tensor<T> Channel<T>::observation(std::ptrdiff_t i) const
386  {
387    const auto n = static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(size());
388    if (i < -n || i >= n)
389    {
390      throw std::out_of_range(
391          "Observation index " + std::to_string(i) + " is out of range for "
392          + std::to_string(n) + " observations");
393    }
394    Tensor<T> out(shape());
395    copy_observation(static_cast<size_t>(i < 0 ? i + n : i), out.data());
396    return out;
397  }

399  template <typename T>
400  template <std::random_access_iterator Out>
401  void Channel<T>::copy_observation(size_t k, Out out) const
402  {
403    if (!m_source)
404    {
405      const auto blockSize = block_size();
406      const auto& stored = m_storage->values;
407      for (size_t v = 0; v < blockSize; ++v)
408      {
409        out[static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(v)] = detail::store_copy(stored[k * blockSize + v]);
410      }
411      return;
412    }
413    const auto sourceBlock = m_source->block_size();
414    for (size_t j = 0; j < m_lags.size(); ++j)
415    {
416      m_source->copy_observation(source_index(k, j), out + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(j * sourceBlock));
417    }
418  }

438  template <typename T>
439  std::vector<size_t> Channel<T>::indices_sorted(std::span<const float64_t> sortedTimes) const
440  {
441    std::vector<size_t> indices;
442    indices.reserve(sortedTimes.size());
443    size_t hint = 0;
444    for (const auto t : sortedTimes)
445    {
446      hint = index_at(t, hint);
447      indices.push_back(hint);
448    }
449    return indices;
450  }
451
452  template <typename T>
453  size_t Channel<T>::index_at(float64_t t, size_t hint) const
454  {
455    // Gallop from the hint to the first timestamp after t, so a query near
456    // the hint costs a comparison or two and a distant one O(log n).
457    const auto& times = held_times().values();
458    const auto begin = times.begin();
459    const auto from = begin + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(std::min(hint, times.size()));
460    return snap(static_cast<size_t>(gallop_upper_bound(begin, times.end(), from, t) - begin), t);
461  }
462
463  template <typename T>
464  size_t Channel<T>::snap(size_t next, float64_t t) const
465  {
466    const auto& times = held_times().values();
467    if (next == 0)
468    {
469      return 0;
470    }
471    // A query computed in floating point, such as 0.3 - 0.1, can land just
472    // below the observation it means; snap it forward. As in the tolerant
473    // comparison of APL, the distance is relative to the magnitude of the
474    // timestamps, so it scales with their rounding errors and does not
475    // depend on the unit. On large timestamps close together, it can exceed
476    // the gap, so that even a query at the previous timestamp would snap past
477    // the observation recorded there; capping it at a quarter of the gap
478    // keeps a query nearer the previous observation where it is.
Possible data race during read of size 8 at 0x35AD4F88 by thread #16

460    return snap(static_cast<size_t>(gallop_upper_bound(begin, times.end(), from, t) - begin), t);
461  }

63  template<typename _Res, typename _MemFun, typename _Tp, typename... _Args>
64    constexpr _Res

479    if (next < times.size() && m_snapTolerance > 0)
480    {
481      const auto prev = times[next - 1];
482      const auto magnitude = std::max(std::abs(prev), std::abs(times[next]));
483      const auto reach = std::min(m_snapTolerance * magnitude, (times[next] - prev) / 4);
484      if (times[next] - t <= reach)
485      {
486        return next;
487      }
488    }
489    return next - 1;
490  }

492  template <typename T>
493  TimeSeries<T>::TimeSeries(std::vector<Channel<T>> channels, TimeUnit unit, std::optional<DateTime> epoch)
494    : m_channels(std::move(channels)), m_unit(unit), m_epoch(epoch)
495  {
496    if (m_channels.empty())
497    {
498      throw std::invalid_argument("A time series needs at least one channel");
499    }
500    check_epoch(m_unit, m_epoch);
501  }
502
503  template <typename T>
504  TimeSeries<T> TimeSeries<T>::from_channels(const std::vector<TimeSeries>& parts, std::optional<TimeUnit> unit)
505  {
506    if (parts.empty())
507    {
508      throw std::invalid_argument("A time series needs at least one channel");
509    }
510    const auto& front = parts.front();
511    const auto target = unit.value_or(front.unit());
512    std::vector<Channel<T>> channels;
513    channels.reserve(parts.size());
514    for (const auto& part : parts)
515    {
516      if (part.n_channels() != 1)
517      {
518        throw std::invalid_argument("Each channel must be a one-channel series; select channels with series[i]");
519      }
520      check_same_kind_of_unit(part.unit(), front.unit());
521      if (!unit && part.unit() != front.unit())
522      {
523        throw std::invalid_argument("The channels differ in unit; pass unit= to choose one");
524      }
525      check_same_kind_of_unit(part.unit(), target);
526      // One epoch for the series: every channel has the same one, or none.
527      // Python counts dated channels from one epoch before combining them.
528      if (part.epoch() != front.epoch())
529      {
530        throw std::invalid_argument("The channels have different epochs; give every channel an epoch, or none");
531      }
532      channels.push_back(part.m_channels.front().to(part.unit(), target));
533    }
534    return TimeSeries(std::move(channels), target, front.epoch());
535  }
536
537  template <typename T>
538  TimeSeries<T> TimeSeries<T>::select(const std::vector<std::ptrdiff_t>& indices) const
539  {
540    const auto n = static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(m_channels.size());
541    std::vector<Channel<T>> channels;
542    channels.reserve(indices.size());
543    for (const auto i : indices)
544    {
545      if (i < -n || i >= n)
546      {
547        throw std::out_of_range(
548            "Channel index " + std::to_string(i) + " is out of range for " + std::to_string(n) + " channels");
549      }
550      channels.push_back(m_channels[static_cast<size_t>(i < 0 ? i + n : i)]);
551    }
552    return TimeSeries(std::move(channels), m_unit, m_epoch);
553  }
554
555  template <typename T>
556  TimeSeries<T> TimeSeries<T>::to(TimeUnit unit) const
557  {
558    if (unit == TimeUnit::Unitless && m_unit != TimeUnit::Unitless)
559    {
560      throw std::invalid_argument("Times in a unit cannot become plain numbers");
561    }
562    auto series = *this;
563    for (auto& channel : series.m_channels)
564    {
565      channel = channel.to(m_unit, unit);
566    }
567    series.m_unit = unit;
568    return series;
569  }
570
571  template <typename T>
572  TimeSeries<T> TimeSeries<T>::with_epoch(std::optional<DateTime> epoch) const
573  {
574    check_epoch(m_unit, epoch);
575    auto series = *this;
576    series.m_epoch = epoch;
577    return series;
578  }
579
580  template <typename T>
581  TimeSeries<T> TimeSeries<T>::shifted(float64_t delta, std::optional<DateTime> epoch) const
582  {
583    check_epoch(m_unit, epoch);
584    auto series = *this;
585    for (auto& channel : series.m_channels)
586    {
587      channel = channel.shifted(delta);
588    }
589    series.m_epoch = epoch;
590    return series;
591  }
592
593  template <typename T>
594  NestedTensor<T> TimeSeries<T>::evaluate(float64_t t) const
595  {
596    check_observed(t);
597    Tensor<NestedTensor<T>> values({m_channels.size()});
598    for (size_t i = 0; i < m_channels.size(); ++i)
599    {
600      const auto& c = m_channels[i];
601      values(i) = NestedTensor<T>::from_leaf_view(c.lookup(t));
602    }
603    return NestedTensor<T>::from_outer_view(std::move(values), 1);
604  }
605
606  template <typename T>
607  NestedTensor<T> TimeSeries<T>::evaluate(const Tensor<float64_t>& t) const
608  {
609    const auto [queries, order] = detail::sort_queries(t);
610    if (!order.empty())
611    {
612      check_observed(queries[order.front()]);
613      check_observed(queries[order.back()]);
614    }
615
616    Tensor<NestedTensor<T>> values({m_channels.size()});
617    std::vector<float64_t> sorted(queries.size());
618    for (size_t c = 0; c < m_channels.size(); ++c)
619    {
620      const auto& channel = m_channels[c];
621      for (size_t k = 0; k < order.size(); ++k)
622      {
623        sorted[k] = queries[order[k]];
624      }
625      std::vector<size_t> shape(t.shape().begin(), t.shape().end());
626      shape.insert(shape.end(), channel.shape().begin(), channel.shape().end());
627      Tensor<T> out(shape);
628      if (!order.empty())
629      {
630        channel.lookup_sorted(sorted, order, out.data());
631      }
632      values(c) = NestedTensor<T>::from_leaf_view(std::move(out));
633    }
634    return NestedTensor<T>::from_outer_view(std::move(values), 1);
635  }
636
637  template <typename T>
638  typename TimeSeries<T>::Domain TimeSeries<T>::domain() const
639  {
640    Domain domain{-std::numeric_limits<float64_t>::infinity(), std::numeric_limits<float64_t>::infinity(), true,
641                  true};
642    for (const auto& c : m_channels)
643    {
644      if (c.size() == 0)
645      {
646        throw std::domain_error("Cannot evaluate a channel without observations");
647      }
648      // An open end at the same time as another channel's closed one still
649      // excludes that time, and likewise for starts.
650      const auto first = c.time(0);
651      if (first > domain.first || (first == domain.first && !c.start_closed()))
652      {
653        domain.first = first;
654        domain.closedStart = c.start_closed();
655      }
656      const auto last = c.end().value_or(c.time(c.size() - 1));
657      if (last < domain.last || (last == domain.last && !c.end_closed()))
658      {
659        domain.last = last;
660        domain.closedEnd = c.end_closed();
661      }
662    }
663    return domain;
664  }
665
666  template <typename T>
667  void TimeSeries<T>::check_observed(float64_t t) const
668  {
669    const auto domain = this->domain();
670    if (!domain.contains(t))
671    {
672      throw TimeSeriesDomainError("Time " + detail::shortest_text(t) + " is outside the domain " + domain.to_string(),
673                                  t, domain);
674    }
675  }
676
677  inline std::string TimeDomain::to_string() const
678  {
679    return (closedStart ? "[" : "(") + detail::shortest_text(first) + ", " + detail::shortest_text(last)
680           + (closedEnd ? "]" : ")");
681  }
682
683  namespace detail
684  {
685    /// The position of a series in a tensor, as "TimeSeries at position
686    /// (1, 2)".
687    inline std::string position_text(const std::vector<size_t>& index)
688    {
689      std::string position;
690      for (const auto i : index)
691      {
692        position += (position.empty() ? "" : ", ") + std::to_string(i);
693      }
694      return "TimeSeries at position (" + position + ")";
695    }
696
697    /// Every series of @p series evaluated at the queries @p t, where
698    /// time_of(one, c, q) is the time of query q in channel c of the series
699    /// one, increasing in q. check(one) throws for a series that cannot take
700    /// the queries. See evaluate() for the result and errors.
701    template <typename T, typename Q, typename TimeOf, typename Check>
702    NestedTensor<T> evaluate_each(const Tensor<TimeSeries<T>>& series, const Tensor<Q>& t, TimeOf time_of,
703                                  Check check, Executor& exec)
704    {
705      if (series.size() == 0)
706      {
707        throw std::invalid_argument("Cannot evaluate an empty tensor of series");
708      }
709
710      // The queries are sorted once for every series, as TimeSeries::evaluate
711      // sorts them for one.
712      const auto [queries, order] = sort_queries(t);
713
714      // Leaf c holds, in block k, the values of channel c of the k-th series
715      // in row-major order.
716      const auto& first = series(std::vector<size_t>(series.rank(), 0));
717      std::vector<Tensor<T>> leaves;
718      for (size_t c = 0; c < first.n_channels(); ++c)
719      {
720        std::vector<size_t> shape(series.shape().begin(), series.shape().end());
721        shape.insert(shape.end(), t.shape().begin(), t.shape().end());
722        shape.insert(shape.end(), first.channel(c).shape().begin(), first.channel(c).shape().end());
723        leaves.emplace_back(shape);
724      }
725
726      // Check every series in order first, so that an error names the first
727      // series that cannot be evaluated, whichever worker would reach it.
728      walk(series, [&](const std::vector<size_t>& index) {
729        const auto& one = series(index);
730        check(one);
731        if (one.n_channels() != first.n_channels())
732        {
733          throw std::invalid_argument("Every series must have the same number of channels");
734        }
735        for (size_t c = 0; c < one.n_channels(); ++c)
736        {
737          if (one.channel(c).shape() != first.channel(c).shape())
738          {
739            throw std::invalid_argument("Every series must have the same channel shapes");
740          }
741        }
742        if (order.empty())
743        {
744          return;
745        }
746        for (size_t c = 0; c < one.n_channels(); ++c)
747        {
748          // The domain of the series, or of channel c for queries that
749          // depend on the channel.
750          const auto domain = one.n_channels() == 1 ? one.domain() : one.select({static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(c)}).domain();
751          for (const auto query : {queries[order.front()], queries[order.back()]})
752          {
753            const auto x = time_of(one, c, query);
754            if (!domain.contains(x))
755            {
756              throw TimeSeriesDomainError(position_text(index) + ": Time " + shortest_text(x)
757                                          + " is outside the domain " + domain.to_string(), x, domain, index, c);
758            }
759          }
760        }
761      });
762
763      if (!order.empty())
764      {
765        parallel_walk(series, [&](const std::vector<size_t>& index) {
766          size_t k = 0;
767          for (size_t i = 0; i < index.size(); ++i)
768          {
769            k = k * series.shape(i) + index[i];
770          }
771          const auto& one = series(index);
772          thread_local std::vector<float64_t> sorted;
773          sorted.resize(order.size());
774          for (size_t c = 0; c < leaves.size(); ++c)
775          {
776            for (size_t q = 0; q < order.size(); ++q)
777            {

806  template <typename T>
807  float64_t time_of_date(const TimeSeries<T>& series, size_t c, const DateTime& date)
808  {
809    const auto& channel = series.channel(c);
810    const auto offset = time_since(date, *series.epoch(), series.unit());
811    const auto origin = channel.origin();
812    const auto t = offset - origin;
813    if (channel.size() == 0)
814    {
815      return t;
816    }
817    const auto reach = channel.snap_tolerance() * std::max(std::abs(offset), std::abs(origin));
818    const auto start = channel.time(0);
819    if (channel.start_closed() && t < start && start - t <= reach)
820    {
821      return start;
822    }
823    const auto end = channel.end().value_or(channel.time(channel.size() - 1));
824    if (channel.end_closed() && t > end && t - end <= reach)
825    {
826      return end;
827    }
828    return t;
829  }
